evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': 91\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n    92\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n    93\t        \n    94\t        # Evaluate initial population\n int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   106\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   107\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   108\t        \n   109\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   110\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   111\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   112\t\n   113\t        # Main loop\n   114\t        iteration = 0\n   115\t        done = False\n   116\t        \n   117\t        while not done:\n   118\t            iteration += 1\n   119\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   120\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   121\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   122\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   123\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   124\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   125\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   126\t            \n   127\t            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   128\t            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   129\t            \n   130\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   131\t            \n   132\t            # Generate trial vectors\n   133\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   134\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   135\t                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\n   136\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   137\t                if len(candidates) < 3:\n   138\t                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\n   139\t                else:\n   140\t                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n   141\t                \n   142\t                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\n   143\t                \n   144\t                # Mutation and crossover\n   145\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   146\t 163\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   164\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   165\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   166\t                        gbest_f = It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",
    "text": "Here are the available tools:\n```\n{'name': 'bash', 'description': 'Run commands in a bash shell\\n* When invoking this tool, the contents of the \"command\" parameter does NOT need to be XML-escaped.\\n* You don\\'t have access to the internet via this tool.\\n* You do have access to a mirror of common linux and python packages via apt and pip.\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user.\\n* To inspect a particular line range of a file, e.g. lines 10-25, try \\'sed -n 10,25p /path/to/the/file\\'.\\n* Please avoid commands that may produce a very large amount of output.\\n* Please run long lived commands in the background, e.g. \\'sleep 10 &\\' or start a server in the background.', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'description': 'The bash command to run.'}}, 'required': ['command']}}\n\n{'name': 'editor', 'description': 'Custom editing tool for viewing, creating and editing files\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user\\n* If `path` is a file, `view` displays the result of applying `cat -n`. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'view', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py'},\n        \"tool_output\": \"Here's the result of running `cat -n` on /hada/metabbo/meta_learning.py:\n     1\t\"\"\"Meta-learning based ParamController for Differential Evolution.\n     2\t\n     3\tProvides `DQNController` and `DiscreteDQNController` implementing the `ParamController` interface.\n     4\tThe controller wraps a small PyTorch network that maps observations to\n     5\tDE parameter values (F, CR, strategy). It can load/save a model\n     6\tand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport os\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\ttry:\n    12\t    import torch\n    13\t    import torch.nn as nn\n    14\t    import torch.nn.functional as F\n    15\texcept Exception:\n    16\t    torch = None\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom param_controller import ParamController\n    19\t\n    20\timport sys\n    21\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    22\tfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n    23\t\n    24\tif torch is not None:\n    25\t    class _Net(nn.Module):\n    26\t        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n    27\t            super().__init__()\n    28\t            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n    29\t            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    30\t            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n    31\t            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n    32\t            \n    33\t            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n    34\t            if seed is not None:\n    35\t                self._init_weights(seed)\n    36\t\n    37\t        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n    38\t            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n    39\t            g = torch.Generator()\n    40\t            g.manual_seed(seed)\n    41\t            \n    42\t            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

    "content": "<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n    \"tool_input\": {\n        \"command\": \"str_replace\",\n        \"path\": \"/hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\",\n        \"old_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n        \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n\\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n\\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n\\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n\\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # SHADE/JADE-style parameter memory with per-individual F and CR\\n        base_params = dict(self.params)\\n        base_F = float(base_params.get('F', 0.5))\\n        base_CR = float(base_params.get('CR', 0.5))\\n        H = 5  # memory size\\n        M_F = np.full(H, base_F, dtype=float)\\n        M_CR = np.full(H, base_CR, dtype=float)\\n        memory_idx = 0\\n        archive_cap = max(pop_size, 10)\\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=float)\\n        pbest_rate = 0.1\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n\\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n                    # The controller still provides a global bias to the memory\\n                    base_F = float(step_params.get('F', base_F))\\n                    base_CR = float(step_params.get('CR', base_CR))\\n\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n            # JADE/SHADE current-to-pbest/1 with archive and adaptive F/CR\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_num = max(2, int(np.ceil(pbest_rate * pop_size)))\\n\\n            S_F = []\\n            S_CR = []\\n            S_improve = []\\n\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n\\n                # Draw per-individual F and CR from memory\\n                mi = int(rng.randint(0, H))\\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[mi] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[mi] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = 0.1 * rng.random() + 1e-9\\n                if F_i > 1.0:\\n                    F_i = 1.0\\n\\n                # pbest individual for current-to-pbest mutation\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[int(rng.randint(0, pbest_num))])\\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n\\n                # random distinct individual r1 from population\\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\\n                attempts = 0\\n                while r1 == i and attempts < 10:\\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\\n                    attempts += 1\\n\\n                # r2 from population + archive\\n                union = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n                n_union = len(union)\\n                r2 = int(rng.randint(0, n_union))\\n                attempts = 0\\n                # Make sure r2 is not the same individual as i or r1\\n                while attempts < 10 and ((r2 == i) or (r2 == r1)):\\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(0, n_union))\\n                    attempts += 1\\n\\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\\n                x_r2 = union[r2]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: reflection then final clip\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if mutant[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        mutant[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - mutant[j]\\n                    elif mutant[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        mutant[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - mutant[j]\\n                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\\n\\n                old_f = fitness[i]\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\\n                evals += 1\\n\\n                if trial_f < old_f:\\n                    # Add discarded solution to archive\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i][None, :]])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive_cap))] = pop[i]\\n\\n                    pop[i] = trial\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\\n                    S_improve.append(max(old_f - trial_f, 0.0))\\n\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trial.copy()\\n\\n            # Update F/CR memory using successful parameters\\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\\n                S_F = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\\n                S_CR = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\\n                weights = np.asarray(S_improve, dtype=float)\\n                w_sum = float(np.sum(weights))\\n                if w_sum > 0:\\n                    # Lehmer mean for F, weighted arithmetic mean for CR\\n                    denom = float(np.sum(weights * S_F))\\n                    mean_F = float(np.sum(weights * S_F * S_F) / denom) if denom > 0 else float(np.mean(S_F))\\n                    mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * S_CR) / w_sum)\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F))\\n                    mean_CR = float(np.mean(S_CR))\\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_F):\\n                    mean_F = base_F\\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_CR):\\n                    mean_CR = base_CR\\n                M_F[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                M_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % H\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n\\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n\\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n</json>",

Lehmer mean for F, weighted arithmetic mean for CR\\n                    denom = float(np.sum(weights * S_F))\\n                    mean_F = float(np.sum(weights * S_F * S_F) / denom) if denom > 0 else float(np.mean(S_F))\\n                    mean_CR = float(np.sum(weights * S_CR) / w_sum)\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F))\\n                    mean_CR = float(np.mean(S_CR))\\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_F):\\n                    mean_F = base_F\\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_CR):\\n                    mean_CR = base_CR\\n                M_F[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                M_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % H\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n\\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n\\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n</json>"

p-best individual for mutation\\n                pbest_idx = int(order[int(rng.randint(0, pbest_num))])\\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n\\n                # Random distinct individual r1 from population\\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\\n                attempts = 0\\n                while r1 == i and attempts < 10:\\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\\n                    attempts += 1\\n\\n                # r2 chosen from population + archive\\n                if len(archive) > 0:\\n                    union = np.vstack([pop, archive])\\n                else:\\n                    union = pop\\n                n_union = len(union)\\n                r2 = int(rng.randint(0, n_union))\\n                attempts = 0\\n                while attempts < 10 and (r2 == i or (r2 < pop_size and r2 == r1)):\\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(0, n_union))\\n                    attempts += 1\\n                if r2 >= pop_size:\\n                    # archive entry; duplicate handling is acceptable\\n                    pass\\n\\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\\n                x_r2 = union[r2]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: reflection then clip\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if mutant[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        mutant[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - mutant[j]\\n                    elif mutant[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        mutant[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - mutant[j]\\n                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\\n\\n                old_f = fitness[i]\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\\n                evals += 1\\n\\n                if trial_f < old_f:\\n                    # Add discarded parent to archive\\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i][None, :]])\\n                    else:\\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive_cap))] = pop[i]\\n\\n                    pop[i] = trial.copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\\n                    S_improve.append(float(old_f - trial_f))\\n\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trial.copy()\\n\\n            # Update F/CR memory using successful parameters (weighted Lehmer/mean)\\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\\n                S_F_arr = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\\n                S_CR_arr = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\\n                w_arr = np.asarray(S_improve, dtype=float)\\n                w_sum = float(np.sum(w_arr))\\n                if w_sum > 0:\\n                    denom = float(np.sum(w_arr * S_F_arr))\\n                    if denom > 1e-12:\\n                        mean_F = float(np.sum(w_arr * S_F_arr * S_F_arr) / denom)\\n                    else:\\n                        mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\\n                    mean_CR = float(np.sum(w_arr * S_CR_arr) / w_sum)\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\\n                    mean_CR = float(np.mean(S_CR_arr))\\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_F):\\n                    mean_F = F_global\\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_CR):\\n                    mean_CR = CR_global\\n                M_F[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                M_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % H\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n\\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n\\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",

archive entry; duplicate handling is acceptable\n   192\t                    pass\n   193\t\n   194\t                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n   195\t                x_r2 = union[r2]\n   196\t\n   197\t                # return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # SHADE/JADE-style adaptive parameter memory and archive\n        H = 5\n        M_F = np.full(H, float(self.params.get('F', 0.5)), dtype=float)\n        M_CR = np.full(H, float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5)), dtype=float)\n        memory_idx = 0\n        archive_cap = max(pop_size, 10)\n        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim), dtype=float)\n        pbest_rate = 0.1\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            F_global = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR_global = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n                    if 'F' in step_params:\n                        F_global = float(step_params['F'])\n                    if 'CR' in step_params:\n                        CR_global = float(step_params['CR'])\n\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n            # Sort individuals by fitness for pbest selection\n            order = np.argsort(fitness)\n            pbest_num = max(2, int(np.ceil(pbest_rate * pop_size)))\n\n            # Collections of successful parameter values for memory update\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n            S_improve = []\n\n            # Generate and evaluate trial vectors (JADE current-to-pbest/1)\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR (SHADE-style)\n                mi = int(rng.randint(0, H))\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(M_CR[mi] + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                F_i = float(np.clip(M_F[mi] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.0, 1.0))\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\n                    F_i = 0.1 * rng.random() + 1e-9\n                if F_i > 1.0:\n                    F_i = 1.0\n\n                # p-best individual for mutation\n                pbest_idx = int(order[int(rng.randint(0, pbest_num))])\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n\n                # Random distinct individual r1 from population\n                r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                attempts = 0\n                while r1 == i and attempts < 10:\n                    r1 = int(rng.randint(0, pop_size))\n                    attempts += 1\n\n                # r2 chosen from population + archive\n                if len(archive) > 0:\n                    union = np.vstack([pop, archive])\n                else:\n                    union = pop\n                n_union = len(union)\n                r2 = int(rng.randint(0, n_union))\n                attempts = 0\n                while attempts < 10 and (r2 == i or (r2 < pop_size and r2 == r1)):\n                    r2 = int(rng.randint(0, n_union))\n                    attempts += 1\n                if r2 >= pop_size:\n                    # archive entry; duplicate handling is acceptable\n                    pass\n\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n                x_r2 = union[r2]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (x_pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Boundary handling: reflection then clip\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if mutant[j] < lb[j]:\n                        mutant[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - mutant[j]\n                    elif mutant[j] > ub[j]:\n                        mutant[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - mutant[j]\n                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(0, self.dim))\n                trial = np.copy(pop[i])\n                for j in range(self.dim):\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\n                        trial[j] = mutant[j]\n\n                old_f = fitness[i]\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < old_f:\n                    # Add discarded parent to archive\n                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i][None, :]])\n                    else:\n                        archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive_cap))] = pop[i]\n\n                    pop[i] = trial.copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    S_improve.append(float(old_f - trial_f))\n\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = trial.copy()\n\n            # Update F/CR memory using successful parameters (weighted Lehmer/mean)\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                S_F_arr = np.asarray(S_F, dtype=float)\n                S_CR_arr = np.asarray(S_CR, dtype=float)\n                w_arr = np.asarray(S_improve, dtype=float)\n                w_sum = float(np.sum(w_arr))\n                if w_sum > 0:\n                    denom = float(np.sum(w_arr * S_F_arr))\n                    if denom > 1e-12:\n                        mean_F = float(np.sum(w_arr * S_F_arr * S_F_arr) / denom)\n                    else:\n                        mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n                    mean_CR = float(np.sum(w_arr * S_CR_arr) / w_sum)\n                else:\n                    mean_F = float(np.mean(S_F_arr))\n                    mean_CR = float(np.mean(S_CR_arr))\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_F):\n                    mean_F = F_global\n                if not np.isfinite(mean_CR):\n                    mean_CR = CR_global\n                M_F[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_F, 0.0, 1.0))\n                M_CR[memory_idx] = float(np.clip(mean_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n                memory_idx = (memory_idx + 1) % H\n\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",
